localhost:5555\n - localhost:5557\n @return {Array}\n */\nfunction getListenAddress(listen, logger) {\n  debug('getListenAddress called with %o', listen);\n  if (!listen) {\n    debug('No listen address provided, using default');\n    return {\n      proto: _core.DEFAULT_PROTOCOL,\n      host: _core.DEFAULT_DOMAIN,\n      port: _core.DEFAULT_PORT\n    };\n  }\n  if (Array.isArray(listen)) {\n    const filteredListen = listen.filter(item => typeof item === 'string');\n    if (filteredListen.length === 0) {\n      throw new Error('Listen addresses array cannot be empty');\n    }\n    const invalid = [];\n    const valid = [];\n    for (const raw of filteredListen) {\n      const candidate = parseAddress(raw);\n      if (candidate) {\n        debug('valid listen address found: %o', candidate);\n        valid.push(candidate);\n      } else {\n        debug('invalid address found: %o', raw);\n        invalid.push(raw);\n      }\n    }\n    invalid.forEach(bad => logger.warn({\n      addr: bad\n    }, 'invalid address - ${listen}, we expect a port (e.g. \"4873\"), ` + `host:port (e.g. \"localhost:4873\"), full url ` + `(e.g. \"http://localhost:4873/\") or unix:/path/socket`);\n  }\n  return single;\n}\n//# sourceMappingURL=address.js.map","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":111,"totalLines":111,"truncated":false}},"title":"node_modules/.pnpm/@verdaccio+config@9.0.0-next-9.1/node_modules/@verdaccio/config/build/address.js","time":{"start":1782057632550,"end":1782057632583}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_0c607a70abfb07d7016a380a9f5f70819794ce63d48ebc939c"}},"id":"prt_eeae97f25001o7a0juNVOZgEqd","sessionID":"ses_1151b82acffesJzr9rSFzaPxQi","messageID":"msg_eeae96105001BtQrorJHMuC5Fo"}}
